# Self-Guided Japan（selfguidejapan.com） 标准行程（Package Tour 格式）

格式沿用 WEBUY 行程 house grammar：`CITY_EN 中文`、`>` 陆路 / `✈` 航班、(2N) 连住、只列有信息的餐食。
所有内容转录自 selfguidejapan.com 各产品页的搜索索引摘要；空白 = 来源未给出，**不是**不包含。

<a id="bf001"></a>
## 【BF001】Japan Golden Route Highlights

**日本黄金路线精华** · 7天6晚 · USD 1,200–1,800 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 1900–2900; EUR 1000–1600）

- **系列**：BF 基础精选（7天）
- **主题**：Golden Route、World Heritage & National Treasures
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > KYOTO 京都 (2N) > OSAKA 大阪 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Exceptional value for your Japan adventure—quality experience without the premium price tag ✦ Comfortable Travel with Shinkansen from Tokyo to Kyoto (reserved ordinary seat) ✦ Discover Tokyo, Kyoto & Osaka—Japan's top 3 destinations in one great-value package ✦ Convenient luggage delivery between Tokyo → Osaka
- **酒店/旅馆**：Mitsui Garden Hotel Shiodome Italia-gai or similar (Tokyo Shiodome, 2N)、Urban Hotel Kyoto Shijo Premium or similar (Kyoto, 2N)、hotel it. osaka shinmachi or similar (Osaka Nishinagahori, 2N)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance、Shinkansen for major intercity travel、Transportation IC card (JPY3,000)、Accommodation for 6 nights、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Osaka) · **不含**：Hotel breakfast、International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax
- **来源**：https://selfguidejapan.com/tours/BF001（可信度 high）

| Day | 路线 | 交通 | 景点 / 活动 | 餐 | 住宿 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | TOKYO 东京　Welcome to Japan- Arrival in Tokyo |  | Welcome to Japan! Our staff will be waiting for you at Haneda/Narita Airport. We will provide you with everything you need for this trip and escort you to the taxi/bus stand or train station. Hotel Check-in |  | 东京 |
| 2 | TOKYO 东京　Sightseeing in Tokyo |  | Asakusa、TOKYO SKYTREE、Imperial Palace、Shibuya Scramble Crossing、Harajuku ✦ This vibrant city beautifully blends traditional Japanese culture with the latest trends. The best way to experience all that Tokyo has to offer is by exploring on foot, so put on your walking shoes and get ready for an adventure! |  | 东京 |
| 3 | TOKYO 东京 > KYOTO 京都　Transfer to Kyoto + sightseeing in Kyoto | 🚅Tokyo Station→Kyoto Station | Ninenzaka、Sannenzaka、Kiyomizu-dera Temple、Gion、Yasaka Shrine、Kamo River ✦ Check out and send your large luggage to Osaka. This will help you explore Kyoto more easily. Travel from Tokyo Station to Kyoto by Shinkansen in the morning. Kyoto is the heart of Japanese culture, filled with historic temples, shrines, and beautiful streets. Hotel Check-in |  | 京都 |
| 4 | KYOTO 京都　Sightseeing in Kyoto |  | Kinkaku-ji Temple、Arashiyama、Fushimi Inari Taisha Shrine、Nishiki Market、Kawaramachi ✦ Today, we will take a longer trip to explore Kyoto’s famous sights. |  | 京都 |
| 5 | KYOTO 京都 > OSAKA 大阪　Transfer to Osaka + sightseeing in Osaka | 🚃Kyoto Station→Osaka Station | Tennoji、Abeno Harukas、Shinsekai、Umeda Sky Building / Sky Garden Observatory ✦ Hotel Check out Take train to Osaka (In the morning) (using an IC Card Suica) Osaka is a lively city known for its busy downtown areas and varied food culture. It has many attractions, from historical sites to modern viewpoints. Hotel Check-in |  | 大阪 |
| 6 | OSAKA 大阪　Sightseeing in Osaka |  | Nakanoshima Area、Osaka Castle、Dotonbori、Shinsaibashi ✦ Day 2 in Osaka: More Tourist Spots to Explore! |  | 大阪 |

| 4 | KASHIKOJIMA 贤岛　Choose Your Ise Adventure Day |  | Breakfast at the Hotel Today, please choose one of the two activities below that interests you the most. [A] Minami-Ise Fishing Village Fishing Experience Tour Cook salt-baked fish caught in Asoura, a traditional fishing village. You will also tour a fish farm with local fishermen. Enjoy fishing in the waters of Minami-Ise. [B] Shima Green Adventure and Ama Hut Tour Get active and explore the natural forest. This option is great for families with young children. For lunch, you will experience the ama culture, which has been part of Ise for over 2,000 years. An Ise ama will grill fish right in front of you. After the activities, return to the hotel. Dinner at the Hotel | BLD | 贤岛 |
| 5 | KASHIKOJIMA 贤岛 > KYOTO 京都　Ninja Training Experience in Iga |  | Akame 48 Falls、Ninja Training Village ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Hotel Check-out In the afternoon, travel from Kashikojima to Nabari by limited express train, then take a chartered taxi to Akame. Akame is a tranquil mountain retreat, where lush forests, beautiful waterfalls, and fascinating ninja history come together. Enjoy sightseeing in Akame. After sightseeing, take a chartered taxi to Nabari, then transfer to Kyoto by limited express train. Hotel Check-in | BL | 京都 |
| 6 | KYOTO 京都　Exploring Kyoto's Historic Streets |  | Gion、Yasaka Shrine、Pontocho、Kiyomizu-dera Temple、Ninenzaka、Sannenzaka、Kamo River ✦ Kyoto is the heart of Japanese culture, filled with historic temples, shrines, and beautiful streets. Enjoy sightseeing in Kyoto. |  | 京都 |

Shop at Osaka's foremost shopping districts Dotonbori and Tsutenkaku Tower and indulge in takoyaki and okonomiyaki until your heart's content! For a change of pace, why not visit Osaka Aquarium Kaiyukan and discover the fascinating world beneath the sea? Nara is the place to be. Nara predates Kyoto as an ancient capital, and its UNESCO World Heritage Sites — Todaiji Temple, Kasuga Taisha Shrine, and Kofukuji Temple — are just some of many spots with a rich and immersive history. Nara Park also provides a unique experience where feeding the gentle deer offers a moment to connect with nature. |  | 京都 |

**日本秘境：伊势志摩之旅** · 12天11晚 · USD 3,300–4,400 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 5500–7200; EUR 2900–3900）

- **亮点**：✦ Comfortable Travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Let's enjoy an Ama hut experience with fresh seafood BBQ grilled by local women divers, combined with the Kashikojima España Cruise to take in the stunning scenery of Ago Bay ✦ Visit Koyasan, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, known for the spiritual center of Shingon Buddhism and the mystical atmosphere of Mount Koya ✦ Convenient luggage delivery between Tokyo → Osaka, Osaka → Tokyo
- **酒店/旅馆**：Hotel Monterey La Soeur Ginza or similar (Tokyo Ginza, 2N, standard)、The Cypresses Mercure Nagoya or similar (Nagoya, 1N, standard)、Miyako Resort Shima Bayside Terrace  (Ise-Shima, 1N, standard)、Hotel Monterey Grasmere Osaka or similar (Osaka Namba, 3N, standard)、Miyako Hotel Kyoto Hachijo or similar (Kyoto, 2N, standard)、Hotel Monterey La Soeur Ginza or similar (Tokyo Ginza, 2N, standard)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance 、Shinkansen, Kintetsu limited express and Nankai limited express trains for major intercity travel、Accommodation for 11 nights with breakfast and 1 dinner、Ama hut experience with lunch and shuttle bus (Day4)、Kashikojima cruise、Transportation IC card (JPY13,000)、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Osaka  Osaka→Tokyo) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax

Toba is a port town in Mie Prefecture. It is known for its beautiful sea and island views and for its Ama diving culture. From Toba, take the shuttle bus to Hachiman Kamado. Hachiman Kamado is an Ama hut where you can enjoy a meal while Ama divers share stories about their fishing experiences and lives. You will enjoy delicious Ise seafood for lunch! **日本秘境：伊势志摩之旅（豪华版）** · 12天11晚 · USD 5,900–7,600 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 9700–12500; EUR 5200–6700）

- **亮点**：✦ Comfortable Travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Let's enjoy an Ama hut experience with fresh seafood BBQ grilled by local women divers, combined with the Kashikojima España Cruise to take in the stunning scenery of Ago Bay ✦ Visit Koyasan, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, known for the spiritual center of Shingon Buddhism and the mystical atmosphere of Mount Koya ✦ Convenient luggage delivery between Tokyo → Osaka, Osaka → Tokyo
- **酒店/旅馆**：Sheraton Miyako Hotel Tokyo (Tokyo Shirokanedai, 2N, deluxe)、Nagoya Marriott Associa Hotel or similar (Nagoya, 1N, deluxe)、Shima Kanko Hotel The Classic (Kashikojima, 1N, deluxe)、Osaka Marriott Miyako Hotel or similar (Osaka Tennoji, 3N, deluxe)、The Westin Miyako Kyoto or similar (Kyoto, 2N, deluxe)、Sheraton Miyako Hotel Tokyo (Tokyo Shirokanedai, 2N, deluxe)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance 、Shinkansen, Kintetsu limited express and Nankai limited express trains for major intercity travel、Accommodation for 11 nights with breakfast and 1 dinner、Ama hut experience with lunch and shuttle bus、Kashikojima cruise、Transportation IC card (JPY13,000)、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Osaka  Osaka→Tokyo) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax

Toba is a port town in Mie Prefecture. It is known for its beautiful sea and island views and for its Ama diving culture. From Toba, take the shuttle bus to Hachiman Kamado. Hachiman Kamado is an Ama hut where you can enjoy a meal while Ama divers share stories about their fishing experiences and lives. You will enjoy delicious Ise seafood for lunch! Koyasan is a mountain town recognized as a UNESCO World Heritage Site. It is surrounded by cedar forests and has deep ties to Japanese Buddhism. - **酒店/旅馆**：Hotel Monterey Edelhof Sapporo (Sapporo, 2N)、Sounkyo Onsen Hotel Taisetsu (Sounkyo, 1N)、TSURUGA Akan Yuku no Sato (Akan, 3N)

| Day | 路线 | 交通 | 景点 / 活动 | 餐 | 住宿 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SAPPORO 札幌　Welcome to Japan- Arrival in Hokkaido |  | Welcome to Japan! Our staff awaits you at New Chitose Airport with everything needed for your journey. Enjoy a comfortable chartered taxi transfer to your hotel for check-in. |  | 札幌 |
| 2 | SAPPORO 札幌　A day exploring Sapporo and the charm of Otaru |  | Hotel breakfast Spend a full day exploring vibrant Sapporo, renowned for its spacious urban layout, abundant nature, and exceptional Hokkaido cuisine. Must-visit attractions include Odori Park, the city's central green space hosting seasonal festivals and offering stunning cityscapes; Susukino, Sapporo's largest entertainment district perfect for experiencing local nightlife and gourmet dining; and the iconic Sapporo Clock Tower, a historic landmark showcasing Hokkaido's pioneering heritage. (*Entrance fees not included) Consider extending your adventure to picturesque Otaru, a charming historic port town featuring preserved stone warehouses and romantic canals. Highlights include the famous Otaru Canal with its nostalgic stone buildings, Kitaichi Glass for exquisite handcrafted glassware, the enchanting Music Box Museum, and Sankaku Market for the freshest seafood experiences. | B | 札幌 |
| 3 | SAPPORO 札幌 > SOUNKYO 层云峡　Wildlife encounters and scenic wonders in Sounkyo Gorge |  | Enjoy the Hotel breakfast and checkout. Travel by chartered taxi to Asahikawa visiting Asahiyama Zoo, Hokkaido's premier wildlife park for observing natural animal behaviors up close. (*Entrance fees not included) Continue to Sounkyo Gorge in Daisetsuzan National Park, featuring dramatic cliffs, waterfalls, and seasonal landscapes. Witness the contrasting Ginga and Ryusei Falls, Sounkyo's iconic twin waterfalls offering breathtaking scenery. Transfer to hotel via chartered taxi for check-in and dinner. | BD | 层云峡 |
| 4 | SOUNKYO 层云峡 > LAKE AKAN 阿寒湖　A scenic journey through peaks, lakes, and open skies |  | Enjoy the Hotel breakfast and checkout. Take chartered taxi to Kurodake Ropeway, ascending through spectacular gorge and forest views via cable car and chairlift for breathtaking mountain panoramas. (*Entrance fees not included) Enjoy free lunch in Kitami, then continue to Bihoro Pass, offering stunning overlook views of Lake Kussharo, one of Hokkaido's most iconic scenic spots. Explore Japan's largest caldera lake, where vast tranquil waters and surrounding wilderness create serene natural beauty. Return to hotel via chartered taxi for check-in and dinner. | BD | 阿寒湖 |
| 5 | LAKE AKAN 阿寒湖　Discovering the untamed beauty of Shiretoko and Lake Mashu |  | Hotel breakfast Journey by chartered taxi to Shiretoko, a UNESCO World Heritage site at Hokkaido's northeastern tip, renowned for pristine wilderness, diverse wildlife, and spectacular landscapes. Key destinations include: Shiretoko Five Lakes, featuring pristine forests, serene lakes, and mountain views accessible via elevated boardwalks; Shiretoko Nature Center for wildlife education and recommended lunch spot; and Shiretoko Pass, one of the "Eight Views of Shiretoko," offering magnificent vistas of Mount Rausu and dramatic mountain scenery. Return to hotel via chartered taxi for dinner. | BD | 阿寒湖 |
| 6 | LAKE AKAN 阿寒湖　Relaxing moments by the peaceful shores of Lake Akan |  | Hotel breakfast Enjoy free time exploring Lake Akan area, where rich natural beauty meets indigenous Ainu culture. Experience the serene Lake Akan cruise offering stunning views of Mount O-Akan and pristine lakeside scenery. Visit Ainu Kotan village to discover traditional culture, crafts, and performances in authentic shops and theaters. Stroll the Bokke Nature Trail through forests to witness fascinating mud volcanoes created by geothermal activity.(*Entrance fees not included) Perfect blend of natural wonders and cultural heritage. Hotel dinner | BD | 阿寒湖 |
| 7 | LAKE AKAN 阿寒湖　Sayonara – Until We Meet Again in Japan! |  | Hotel breakfast The tour ends at hotel check-out. Thank you for spending the last 7 days with our tour. We hope to see you again! | B |  |

Recommended highlights include: Lake Toya cruise offering tranquil views of Nakajima Island and surrounding peaks from the water; Mount Usu Ropeway ascending to panoramic viewpoints overlooking the lake and dramatic volcanic landscape; and Silo Observatory, a popular scenic spot providing breathtaking views of Lake Toya and its central island and complete the day with a Hotel dinner (Entrance fees not included). | BD | 洞爷湖 |

Discover Hakodate, southern Hokkaido's charming port city celebrated for stunning ocean vistas, historic streetscapes, and exceptional fresh seafood. Must-visit attractions include: Kanemori Red Brick Warehouses, beautifully preserved historical buildings now housing delightful shops and cafes; scenic Bay Area strolls along the harbor showcasing Hakodate's nostalgic maritime charm; Hachiman-zaka slope offering iconic city views stretching toward the sea; and Mount Hakodate's world-renowned night panorama accessible via ropeway, considered among Japan's most spectacular evening vistas. (*Entrance fees not included) Hotel check-in | B | 函馆 |

Ascend Goryokaku Tower for spectacular aerial views of the distinctive pentagonal layout and panoramic city vistas. Motomachi District - Stroll charming hillside streets lined with historic churches and elegant Western-style mansions, immersing yourself in Hakodate's rich cultural heritage and architectural treasures from the Meiji era. ## 【HS001】Healing Waters & Timeless Towns: Onsen Journey Across Japan

**疗愈之泉与永恒小镇：日本温泉之旅** · 14天13晚 · USD 4,400–5,900 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 7200–9700; EUR 3800–5200）

- **系列**：HS 温泉之旅
- **主题**：Onsen & Relaxation
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > HIMEJI 姬路 (1N) > HIROSHIMA 广岛 (2N) > KYOTO 京都 (2N) > KANAZAWA 金泽 (2N) > SHIRAKAWA-GO 白川乡 (1N) > GERO 下吕温泉 (1N) > TOKYO 东京 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Explore Japan's Most Loved Cities and Relaxing Hot Springs Tour ✦ Enjoy a comfortable 14-day journey featuring reserved-seat Shinkansen and limited express trains, along with chartered sightseeing taxis. ✦ A tour of three UNESCO World Heritage Sites: Himeji Castle, Itsukushima Shrine, and Shirakawa-go. ✦ In Kanazawa, Shirakawa-go, and Gero, enjoy soothing hot springs and traditional Japanese kaiseki dinners. ✦ In Hiroshima, the package includes high-speed ferry tickets to Miyajima, departing from your hotel. ✦ Enjoy a day trip to Takayama from Shirakawa-go with a chartered sightseeing taxi. ✦ Convenient luggage delivery between Tokyo → Hiroshima, Hiroshima → Kanazawa, Kanazawa → Tokyo
- **酒店/旅馆**：Hotel New Otani Tokyo (Tokyo Akasaka-mitsuke, 2N, onsen plan)、Hotel Monterey Himeji (Himeji, 1N, onsen plan)、Grand Prince Hotel Hiroshima  (Hiroshima , 2N, onsen plan)、RIHGA Royal Hotel Kyoto or similar (Kyoto, 2N, onsen plan)、THE HOTEL SANRAKU KANAZAWA  (Kanazawa , 2N, onsen plan)、Onyado Yui no Sho  (Shirakawa-go, 1N, onsen plan)、Koyoi Tenku Ni Asobu Shogetsu  (Gero, 1N, onsen plan)、Hotel New Otani Tokyo (Tokyo Akasaka-mitsuke, 2N, onsen plan)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance、Shinkansen, Limited Express trains for major intercity travel、Transportation IC card（JPY5,000）、High speed ferry to Miyajima 、Chartered taxi from Kanazawa to Shirakawa-go、Chartered taxi from Shirakawa-go to Takayama (including 4-hour Takayama sightseeing)、Accommodation for 13 nights with breakfast and 2 dinners、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Hiroshima, Hiroshima→Kanazawa, Kanazawa→Tokyo) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax

- **亮点**：✦ A trip to Koyasan, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, the spiritual heartland of Shingon Buddhism, where an air of mystery lingers. ✦ Explore Kyoto's golden Kinkakuji Temple and thousand torii gates at Fushimi Inari ✦ Comfortable travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Luggage delivery service from Tokyo to Kyoto and Kyoto to Tokyo for convenient travel

| 2 | OSAKA 大阪　Cherry Blossom Trails of Nara by Kintetsu Railway |  | Breakfast at the hotel Today, let’s head out to enjoy the beautiful cherry blossoms of Nara. Travel by Kintetsu train to Haibara, then continue by bus to Takami-no-Sato, a spectacular mountain garden surrounded by over 1,000 weeping cherry trees. During the spring bloom, the slopes are transformed into a sea of pink, with panoramic views of the surrounding mountains from the garden’s viewpoints. After enjoying the blossoms, return to Haibara and continue by Kintetsu Railway to Hasedera Temple. Known as the “Temple of Flowers,” Hasedera is a historic Buddhist temple surrounded by nature, with a beautiful temple complex built into the mountainside. Its long stairway, wooden Main Hall and seasonal flowers create a particularly atmospheric setting in spring. Return to Osaka by Kintetsu train. | B | 大阪 |
| 3 | OSAKA 大阪　Ancient Nara at Your Own Pace |  | Todai-ji Temple、Nara Park ✦ Breakfast at the hotel Today, travel to Kintetsu Nara Station using your IC card Suica and explore the city independently. For an easier way to see Nara’s highlights, we recommend the Perfect Nara Park Tour, an English-guided sightseeing bus operated by Nara Kotsu. The tour visits Todaiji, Kasuga Taisha and Mount Wakakusa with a professional English-speaking guide. Return to Osaka by Kintetsu train. | B | 大阪 |

<a id="ky001"></a>
## 【KY001】Hidden Kyushu: Nature, Onsen & Culture Adventure

**秘境九州：自然、温泉与文化** · 9天8晚 · USD 2,900–4,000 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 4700–6500; EUR 2500–3500）

- **系列**：KY 九州温泉
- **主题**：Hidden Gems、World Heritage & National Treasures、Onsen & Relaxation、Nature & Outdoors
- **进出点**：Fukuoka ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：FUKUOKA 福冈 (2N) > NAGASAKI 长崎 (2N) > KUMAMOTO 熊本 (1N) > TAKACHIHO 高千穗 (1N) > BEPPU 别府 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Explore Kyushu, a largely undiscovered region. ✦ Enjoy a comfortable 9-day trip with Shinkansen or Limited Express trains (reserved seats), and chartered taxis. ✦ Take a cruise to Gunkanjima, a World Heritage site (landing may be canceled due to weather). ✦ Experience Kumamoto's beautiful Aso region. ✦ Enjoy free time at Beppu's famous Hell Tour hot springs. ✦ Cover all five prefectures of Kyushu on your round-trip journey. ✦ Travel light with our luggage delivery service during train transfers.

- **进出点**：Fukuoka ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出

- **亮点**：✦ Explore Kyushu, a largely undiscovered region. ✦ Enjoy a comfortable 12-day trip with Shinkansen or express trains (reserved seats), and chartered taxis. ✦ Enjoy a luxurious stay at a premium hotel that makes your travel more special. ✦ Take the sightseeing express train in Ibusuki and enjoy views of the sea and Sakurajima ✦ Experience Kumamoto's beautiful Aso region. ✦ Enjoy free time at Beppu's famous Hell Tour hot springs. ✦ Cover all four prefectures of Kyushu on your round-trip journey. ✦ Travel light with our luggage delivery service during train transfers.
- **酒店/旅馆**：YANAGAWA TACHIBANA-TEI OHANA (Yanagawa, 2N)、Ibusuki Hakusuikan (Ibusuki, 1N)、SHIROYAMA HOTEL Kagoshima (Kagoshima, 2N)、Hotel Shikimi (Takachiho, 1N)、ANA InterContinental Beppu Resort & Spa by IHG (Beppu, 2N)、Kotohira Onsen Kaya-Usagi (Hita, 1N)、Miyako Hotel Hakata (Fukuoka, 2N)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance、Shinkansen, Limited Express trains for major intercity travel、Transportation IC card（JPY3,000）、Taxi to transport from Fukuoka Airport to Yanagawa on Day 1、Sightseeing express Ibusuki Tamatebako from Kagoshima Chuo to Ibusuki、Chartered sightseeing taxi on Day 3, 4, 6, 7, 9, and 10、Accommodation for 11 nights with breakfast、Dinner at hotel in Yanagawa (2 times), Ibusuki (1 time), Takachiho (1 time), Beppu (2 times), Hita (1 time)、Luggage delivery service（Yanagawa→Kagoshima, Kagoshima→Beppu) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax

## 【NT004】Alpine Wonders of Central Japan

**中部日本阿尔卑斯奇观** · 12天11晚 · USD 5,100–6,600 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 8400–10800; EUR 4500–5800）

- **系列**：NT 自然徒步
- **主题**：World Heritage & National Treasures、Nature & Outdoors、Onsen & Relaxation
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > YAMANASHI 山梨 (1N) > UTSUKUSHIGAHARA ONSEN 美原高原 (1N) > MATSUMOTO 松本 (1N) > TAKAYAMA 高山 (2N) > TOYAMA 富山 (1N) > YUDANAKA ONSEN 汤田中温泉 (1N) > TOKYO 东京 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Lake Kawaguchi offers beautiful views of Mount Fuji and the Arakura Fuji Sengen Shrine. ✦ Matsumoto Castle, a National Treasure, stands proudly in its historic castle town. ✦ In Chubu Sangaku National Park, you can explore the clear streams and mountain landscapes of Kamikochi. ✦ Visit Shirakawa-go, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, with traditional Gassho-zukuri farmhouses ✦ Experience the stunning mountains by taking the Tateyama Kurobe Alpine Route.
- **酒店/旅馆**：Hotel Monterey La Soeur Ginza or similar (Tokyo Ginza, 2N)、Hotel Sekifu (Yamanashi, 1N)、Shoho (Utsukushigahara Onsen, 1N)、HOTEL BUENA VISTA (Matsumoto, 1N)、MERCURE HOTEL Hida Takayama or similar (Takayama, 2N)、TOYAMA EXCEL HOTEL TOKYU or similar (Toyama, 1N)、Ryokan Biyunoyado (Yudanaka Onsen, 1N)、Hotel Monterey La Soeur Ginza or similar (Tokyo Ginza, 2N)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance 、Shinkansen and limited express trains for major intercity travel、Transportation IC card（JPY5,000）、Chartered sightseeing taxi on Day3, 4, 6, 7&9、All transportation required to travel through the Tateyama Kurobe Alpine Route、Accommodation for 11 nights with breakfast、Dinner at hotel in Isawa Onsen, Utsukushigahara Onsen, Yudanaka Onsen (3 times)、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Matsumoto, Takayama→Tokyo) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax

This makes it a great spot to connect with both wildlife and nature. After your visit, take the limited express train from Yudanaka to Nagano. You have two sightseeing options: Plan A and Plan B. ## 【NT105】Alpine Routes & Cultural Japan

**阿尔卑斯路线与文化日本** · 11天10晚 · USD 3,000–4,000 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 4900–6500; EUR 2600–3500）

- **系列**：NT 自然徒步（豪华酒店版）
- **主题**：Nature & Outdoors
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > MATSUMOTO 松本 (1N) > TOYAMA 富山 (1N) > KANAZAWA 金泽 (2N) > KYOTO 京都 (2N) > OSAKA-UEHOMMACHI 大阪 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Experience the stunning mountains by taking the Tateyama Kurobe Alpine Route. ✦ Walking between snow walls over ten meters tall at their peak is a unique experience and the highlight of this trip. ✦ Visit High-altitude highlights such as Murodo and Mikurigaike Pond ✦ Explore Toyama and Kanazawa freely in the Hokuriku region. ✦ Walk through the temples, shrines, and streets of Kyoto and Osaka at your own pace. ✦ Enjoy a route that combines the beauty of Japan's Alps with famous city sights.
- **酒店/旅馆**：HOTEL GROOVE SHINJUKU, A PARKROYAL Hotel  (Tokyo Shinjuku, 2N)、HOTEL BUENA VISTA (Matsumoto, 1N)、DoubleTree by Hilton Toyama (Toyama, 1N)、ANA CROWNE PLAZA KANAZAWA or similar (Kanazawa, 2N)、KYOTO TOKYU HOTEL or similar (Kyoto, 2N)、SHERATON Miyako Hotel Osaka or similar (Osaka-Uehommachi, 2N)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance、Shinkansen and limited express trains for major intercity travel、Transportation IC card（JPY6,000）、All transportation required to travel through the Tateyama Kurobe Alpine Route、Accommodation for 10 nights with breakfast、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Kanazawa, Kanazawa→Osaka) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax、Day 6: Optional Shirakawa-go Sightseeing Taxi Fare

**艺术濑户内与日本经典温泉** · 12天11晚 · USD 3,200–4,400 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 5200–7200; EUR 2800–3800）

- **亮点**：✦ Tour of Japan's popular cities + art-famous Seto Inland Sea islands ✦ Comfortable 12-day journey using reserved-seat Shinkansen/limited express trains and sightseeing taxis ✦ Includes shuttle bus tour to enjoy nature at Iya Valley's Kazura Bridge ✦ Stay in Takamatsu and hop between islands famous for the Setouchi Triennale ✦ Himeji Castle, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, nicknamed the "White Egret Castle" for its graceful beauty ✦ Experience healing hot springs and Japanese-style kaiseki dinners in Kotohira and Kinosaki ✦ Explore Shodoshima Island by chartered taxi ✦ Luggage delivery service between cities

<a id="sl001"></a>
## 【SL001】SOLO Hidden Gems of Japan: Ise-Shima Escape

**单人·日本秘境：伊势志摩之旅** · 12天11晚 · USD 3,700–4,400 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 6000–7200; EUR 3200–3800）

- **系列**：SL 单人出行 SOLO
- **主题**：Solo (1 person)
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > NAGOYA 名古屋 (1N) > KASHIKOJIMA 贤岛 (1N) > OSAKA 大阪 (3N) > KYOTO 京都 (2N) > TOKYO 东京 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ A solo tour is a travel plan made for single travelers to enjoy a safe and easy trip on their own. ✦ Comfortable Travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Let's enjoy an Ama hut experience with fresh seafood BBQ grilled by local women divers, combined with the Kashikojima España Cruise to take in the stunning scenery of Ago Bay. ✦ Visit Koyasan, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, known for the spiritual center of Shingon Buddhism and the mystical atmosphere of Mount Koya ✦ Convenient luggage delivery between Tokyo → Osaka, Osaka → Tokyo
- **酒店/旅馆**：Via Inn Prime Akasaka or similar (Tokyo Akasaka, 2N)、ibis Styles Nagoya or similar (Nagoya, 1N)、Miyako Resort Shima Bayside Terrace (Kashikojima, 1N)、Hotel Granvia Osaka or similar (Osaka Umeda, 3N)、Miyako Hotel Kyoto Hachijo or similar (Kyoto, 2N)、Via Inn Prime Akasaka or similar (Tokyo Akasaka, 2N)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance、Shinkansen, Kintetsu limited express and Nankai limited express trains for major intercity travel、Accommodation for 11 nights with breakfast and 1 dinner、Ama hut experience with lunch and shuttle bus (Day4)、Kashikojima cruise、Transportation IC card (JPY13,000)、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Osaka  Osaka→Tokyo) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Accommodation Tax

- **亮点**：✦ A “solo tour” is a travel plan made for single travelers to enjoy a safe and easy trip on their own. ✦ Enjoy Hakone Hot Springs, take in the views of Mount Fuji, and explore with the Hakone Free Pass! ✦ Visit the beautiful white Himeji Castle, a UNESCO World Heritage Site ✦ Visit Itsukushima Shrine, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, with its iconic torii gate standing in the sea ✦ Comfortable travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Luggage delivery service from Tokyo to Kyoto and Kyoto to Tokyo

**日本精华与大阪主题乐园** · 12天11晚 · USD 3,200–4,800 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 5200–7900; EUR 2800–4200）

- **亮点**：✦ Comfortable Travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Harajuku, Shibuya, the Imperial Palace, Tokyo Skytree®, and Asakusa all gather in Japan’s capital ✦ Visit Shirakawa-go, a UNESCO World Heritage Site, with traditional Gassho-zukuri farmhouses ✦ Kyoto with Kinkakuji, Nijo Castle, Fushimi Inari’s thousand torii, and Gion’s traditional streets ✦ Universal City Station's theme park features attractions based on movies, games, and beloved characters. ✦ Luggage delivery service from Tokyo to Kyoto and Kyoto to Tokyo for convenient travel

## 【TP101】15 Days Through Japan & Universal City – Deluxe

**15 天纵览日本与环球影城（豪华版）** · 15天14晚 · USD 5,000–6,500 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 8200–10700; EUR 4400–5700）

- **系列**：TP 主题乐园（豪华酒店版）
- **主题**：Theme Parks
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > KASHIKOJIMA 贤岛 (1N) > KYOTO 京都 (3N) > NARA 奈良 (1N) > OSAKA (UNIVERSAL CITY) 大阪 (3N) > TAKAYAMA 高山 (2N) > OSAKA (UNIVERSAL CITY) 大阪 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Comfortable Travel with Shinkansen and limited express trains ✦ Harajuku, Shibuya, the Imperial Palace, Tokyo Skytree®, and Asakusa all gather in Japan’s capital ✦ Kyoto with Kinkakuji, Nijo Castle, Fushimi Inari’s thousand torii, and Gion’s traditional streets ✦ Nara with UNESCO World Heritage Sites of Todaiji's Great Buddha, Kasuga Taisha, and Kofukuji's pagoda, plus deer roaming Nara Park ✦ Universal City Station's theme park features attractions based on movies, games, and beloved characters. ✦ Convenient luggage delivery between Tokyo → Kyoto, Kyoto → Osaka, and Osaka → Tokyo
- **酒店/旅馆**：Hotel New Otani Tokyo  (Tokyo Akasaka-mitsuke, 2N, deluxe)、Shima Kanko Hotel The Classic (Kashikojima, 1N, deluxe)、RIHGA Royal Hotel Kyoto or similar (Kyoto, 3N, deluxe)、Nara Manyo Wakakusa no Yado Mikasa (Nara, 1N, deluxe)、The Park Front Hotel at Universal Studios Japan  (Osaka Universal City, 3N, deluxe)、Hida Hotel Plaza (Takayama, 2N, deluxe)、Hotel New Otani Tokyo  (Osaka Universal City, 2N, deluxe)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance 、Shinkansen and limited express trains for major intercity travel、Accommodation for 14 nights with breakfast and 3 dinners、Kashikojima cruise、Chartered taxi for Shirakawa-go tour、Transportation IC card (JPY11,000)、Luggage delivery service（Tokyo→Kyoto  Kyoto→Osaka  Osaka→Tokyo） · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Admission fee for Universal City、Accommodation Tax
- **来源**：https://selfguidejapan.com/tours/TP101（可信度 high）

| Day | 路线 | 交通 | 景点 / 活动 | 餐 | 住宿 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | TOKYO 东京　Welcome to Japan- Arrival in Tokyo |  | Welcome to Japan! Staff will be waiting for you at Haneda or Narita Airport. There, you will be given a set of essentials for your trip and will be taken to a taxi stand, limousine bus stop, or train station. Hotel Check-in |  | 东京 |
| 2 | TOKYO 东京　The adventure kicks off in vibrant Tokyo |  | Asakusa、Tokyo Skytree、Imperial Palace、Shibuya Scramble Crossing、Harajuku ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel This vibrant city beautifully blends traditional Japanese culture with the latest trends. The best way to experience all that Tokyo has to offer is by exploring on foot, so put on your walking shoes and get ready for an adventure! | B | 东京 |
| 3 | TOKYO 东京 > KASHIKOJIMA 贤岛　Nagoya Exploration and transfer to Kashikojima |  | Nagoya Castle、Noritake Forest、Osu Shopping District ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel After breakfast, check out and send your large luggage to Kyoto. You can explore Nagoya and Kashikojima at your leisure. In the morning, travel from Tokyo to Nagoya by Shinkansen. Nagoya is a beautiful city full of samurai history and unique food. Enjoy sightseeing in Nagoya! In the afternoon, travel from Kintetsu Nagoya to Kashikojima by limited express train. Hotel Check-in Dinner at the Hotel | BD | 贤岛 |
| 4 | KASHIKOJIMA 贤岛 > KYOTO 京都　Refined Shima – Pearls, Sea & Culture and Transfer to Kyoto |  | Mikimoto Pearl Island ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Hotel Check-out In the morning, board the Kashikojima España Cruise, and take in the full beauty of Ago Bay. Around noon, travel from Kashikojima to Toba by limited express train. Travel from Toba to Kyoto by Sightseeing Limited Express "SHIMAKAZE" or another limited express train. Hotel Check-in | B | 京都 |
| 5 | KYOTO 京都　Stepping into Kyoto's Timeless Traditions |  | Kiyomizu-dera Temple、Ninenzaka、Sannenzaka、Gion、Yasaka Shrine、Kamo River ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Kyoto is the heart of Japanese culture, filled with historic temples, shrines, and beautiful streets. Enjoy sightseeing in Kyoto! | B | 京都 |
| 6 | KYOTO 京都　Kyoto Icons: Timeless Sights of the Ancient Capital |  | Kinkaku-ji Temple、Arashiyama、Fushimi Inari Taisha Shrine、Nishiki Market、Kawaramachi ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Let’s continue sightseeing in Kyoto! | B | 京都 |
| 7 | KYOTO 京都 > NARA 奈良　Nara's Sacred Heritage |  | Kasuga Taisha Shrine、Kofuku-ji Temple、Todai-ji Temple、Nara Park ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel After breakfast, check out and send your large luggage to Osaka. You can explore Nara at your leisure. In the morning, travel from Kyoto to Nara by limited express train. Nara is one of Japan's oldest cultural centers, filled with ancient temples, historic shrines, and peaceful parks. Enjoy sightseeing in Nara. Hotel Check-in Dinner at the Hotel | BD | 奈良 |
| 8 | NARA 奈良 > OSAKA 大阪　Making Memories at Universal City, Osaka |  | Breakfast at the Hotel Hotel Check-out In the morning, travel from Kintetsu Nara to Universal City Station by local trains. We recommend spending time at Universal City to enjoy its world-class theme park. It’s packed with attractions based on Hollywood movies and popular video games, along with entertainment where you can meet beloved characters. Thrills, excitement, and smiles await. Enjoy a fun-filled day at the theme park in Universal City, Osaka! Hotel Check-in | B | 大阪 |
| 9 | OSAKA 大阪　Where Movie Magic Comes to Life |  | Breakfast at the Hotel The adventure continues — let's dive back into the magic! | B | 大阪 |
| 10 | OSAKA 大阪　Free Exploration of Vibrant Osaka |  | Dotonbori、Shinsaibashi、Umeda Sky Building、Tennoji、Abeno Harukas、Nakanoshima Area、Osaka Castle、Shinsekai ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Osaka is lively, with busy neighborhoods and a rich food culture. Enjoy sightseeing in Osaka! Of course, enjoying yourselves at the theme park in Universal City to the fullest today is also a great idea! | B | 大阪 |
| 11 | OSAKA 大阪 > TAKAYAMA 高山　Takayama Old Town |  | Takayama Jinya、Old Town / Sanmachi Street、Hida no Sato ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel After breakfast, check out and send your large luggage to Tokyo. You can explore Takayama at your leisure. In the morning, travel from Shin-Osaka to Nagoya by Shinkansen, then take the limited express train to Takayama. Takayama is a historic mountain town with buildings from the Edo period. You can walk around and see traditional houses and local culture. Enjoy sightseeing in Takayama. Hotel Check-in Dinner at the Hotel | BD | 高山 |
| 12 | TAKAYAMA 高山　Shirakawa-go and Timeless Village Life |  | Gassho-zukuri Village、Wada House、Shirakawa-go Observatory ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Take a chartered taxi to Shirakawa-go, a UNESCO World Heritage Site. This traditional farming village showcases Gassho-zukuri thatched houses designed for harsh winters, preserving ancestral wisdom. After sightseeing, take a chartered taxi to Takayama. | B | 高山 |
| 13 | TAKAYAMA 高山 > TOKYO 东京　Morning in Takayama and Night life in Tokyo |  | Miyagawa Asaichi、Nihonbashi/Ginza ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Hotel Check-out Enjoy a morning in Takayama before departure. In the afternoon, travel from Takayama to Nagoya by limited express train, then transfer to Tokyo by Shinkansen. Hotel Check-in Let's also enjoy the night life in Tokyo. | B | 东京 |
| 14 | TOKYO 东京　Have fun in the friendly and relaxed neighborhoods of Tokyo |  | Kamakura、Nikko、Mount Fuji、Lake Kawaguchi ✦ Breakfast at the Hotel Ready to explore beyond Tokyo? Here are some great spots to check out: Plus, there are exciting amusement and theme parks close by for some extra fun! | B | 东京 |
| 15 | TOKYO 东京　Sayonara, and can't wait to see you next time! |  | Breakfast at the Hotel The tour ends at hotel check-out. Thank you for joining the 15-day tour We look forward to seeing you again. | B |  |

## 【TP102】Culture, Nature, and Universal City Adventure – Deluxe

**文化、自然与环球影城冒险（豪华版）** · 12天11晚 · USD 3,300–4,800 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 5400–7900; EUR 2900–4200）

- **系列**：TP 主题乐园（豪华酒店版）
- **主题**：Theme Parks
- **进出点**：Tokyo (Haneda/Narita) ✈ 进 · ? ✈ 出
- **路线**：TOKYO 东京 (2N) > HIROSHIMA 广岛 (2N) > OSAKA (UNIVERSAL CITY) 大阪 (3N) > KYOTO 京都 (2N) > TOKYO 东京 (2N)
- **亮点**：✦ Kyoto with Kinkakuji, Nijo Castle, Fushimi Inari’s thousand torii, and Gion’s traditional charm ✦ Universal City Station's theme park features attractions based on movies, games, and beloved characters. ✦ Osaka with Shinsaibashi, Dotonbori, Tsutenkaku, and tasty food like Takoyaki and Okonomiyaki ✦ Itsukushima Shrine, a vermilion seaside shrine of 1,400 years standing in the sea ✦ Comfortable travel with Shinkansen ✦ Luggage delivery service from Tokyo to Osaka and Osaka to Tokyo for convenient travel
- **酒店/旅馆**：Hotel New Otani Tokyo  (Tokyo, 2N, deluxe)、Sheraton Grand Hiroshima Hotel or similar (Hiroshima, 2N, deluxe)、The Park Front Hotel at Universal Studios Japan (Osaka Universal City, 3N, deluxe)、RIHGA Royal Hotel Kyoto or similar (Kyoto, 2N, deluxe)、Hotel New Otani Tokyo  (Tokyo Akasaka-mitsuke, 2N, deluxe)
- **含**：24/7 customer support (during your stay in Japan)、Travel insurance 、Shinkansen for major intercity travel、Accommodation for 11 nights with breakfast、Transportation IC card (JPY6,000)、Luggage delivery service (Tokyo-Osaka, Osaka-Tokyo) · **不含**：International flights、Transportation not included in the itinerary such as local train and bus、Hotel early check-in/late check-out fees、Hotel incidental charges、Meals and drinks outside the tour itinerary、Entrance fees to places of personal interest or other attractions not detailed in the itinerary、Personal expenses such as telephone, laundry, shopping、Visa not included if required、Admission fee for Universal City、Accommodation Tax

## 【TP103】Japan Highlights & Theme Park in Osaka – Deluxe

**日本精华与大阪主题乐园（豪华版）** · 12天11晚 · USD 3,600–4,900 /人（per person (site catalog price); AUD 5900–8000; EUR 3200–4300）
